\section{Catalogue of the companion repositories}\label{sec:companion-catalogue}

This appendix extends Appendix~\ref{sec:catalogue} to the laboratory's other repositories, at the commits listed in Table~\ref{tab:repos}. Paths are relative to each repository. Rows were compiled by the same procedure as the main catalogue; in crypto-autoresearcher, rows for experiment specifications, evidence records and corrections were extracted from the records themselves, and its claim tiers are quoted as the repository states them. An approved specification is not a result: specifications with no run are listed by name only. Records that are process rather than results (hypotheses, decisions, proposals, hand-offs, goals, questions) and the literature records are counted. Studies that are the same work as one in the main repository say so in their topic.

\art{knowledge/findings/KN-FIND-14bbd9.md}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-09-09 prescribed square-pattern multi-class instances} & diagnostic & Square-pattern construction yields certified multi-class instances at n=6 (9/\allowbreak{}9 planted recovery, \textasciitilde{}9x above draw-route bound); decade growth more than an order of magnitude below x100/\allowbreak{}decade prediction; ECDLP correspondence null by construction. & open\\[1pt]
\art{research/galbraith_open_threads_20260918.md}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-09-18 Galbraith slide threads, open successors} & literature & Repository check of Galbraith ECC slides threads (coset factor bases, torsion quotients, Frobenius, Sarkar-Singh): several are prior art measured out of regime; opens in-regime successors. & current\\[1pt]
\art{experiments/EXP-ECDLP-65f903}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-01 exponent band contains 1/\allowbreak{}5 worksheet} & diagnostic & Two routes output\_\allowbreak{}validated (0.59 s, 0.92 s); arithmetic worksheet (band contains 1/\allowbreak{}5; 64\textasciicircum{}2 \textless{} 64\textasciicircum{}3), no verdict, no evidence record. & current\\[1pt]
\art{experiments/EXP-ECDLP-911c16}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-01 theta coefficient cross-energy remainder worksheet} & diagnostic & Two routes output\_\allowbreak{}validated (1.23 s, 1.01 s); descriptive finite worksheet, no verdict, no evidence record; no headline number in report. & current\\[1pt]
\art{experiments/EXP-GRUMPY-cd7b82}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-03 dual-meter three-class coverage floor on Z/\allowbreak{}l toys (l=101, 257)} & diagnostic & Outcome O-SUPPORT-M12 on PLAIN matching, dual meters (envelope vs covered set) on Z/\allowbreak{}l toys; no break, no exponent, no n\textgreater{}=131 transfer; no evidence record cites it. & current\\[1pt]
\art{experiments/EXP-LHW-b49bc6}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-03 keyed-hash walk sumset millirho} & blocked & Stage 0 integer freeze ok (6 rows); Stage 1 expired at claim TTL (10730 s) before any sumset count: O-IMPEDIMENT. & current\\[1pt]
\art{research/lopsided-relation-20261006}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-06 lopsided batch decomposition seam} & engineering & Lopsided wanted-pairs conversion of batched decomposition is exact (bit-identical relation sets on 3 instances); hash-seam overhead exactly g; payoff only at high arity m\textgreater{}=9. & current\\[1pt]
\art{research/lean_review_evidence_20261018}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-18 Lean theorem evidence review} & tooling & Independent reviews support 19 scoped Lean claims (5 crypto transport, 4 cryptanalysis rho-collision, 10 autoresearcher certificate/\allowbreak{}NCP); 65 of 66 targets re-checked; no performance conclusion. & current\\[1pt]
\art{research/verification}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-22 verification scripts for theory notes} & tooling & 38 check scripts and results for THM notes and Semaev first-fall Lean certificates (e.g. Boolean parity finite certificate replay). & current\\[1pt]
\art{knowledge/leads/ecdlp}\newline {\scriptsize 2026-10-24 ECDLP lead atlas across four repos} & accounting & 147 lead rows (generic 26, ic-binary 21, ic-engine 19, ic-prime 24, transfer 21, structure 17, exotic 19); 971 of 1,031 ECDLP hypotheses non-terminal; best IC rows mostly above rho, exceptions qualified: SAT S/\allowbreak{}rho 0.854 lower bound (conflicts unpriced, n=17), c2pnb & current\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}}
